virtue of her ante date seniority, he is promoted as Major with effect from 22 Jan 207. Also the officer is promoted with 
notional seniority for pay and promotion in the rank of Captain with effect from 22 Jan 20l3 and Major with effect from 
22 Jan 207 and under the provisions of Para 25 (a) & 38 (a) of Territorial Army Regulations 948 (Revised Edition 976) as 
amended vide Government of India, Ministry of Defence letter No. 8()2006/D(GS-) dated 8 Apr 2006 consequent to 
recommendations of AVSC Committee report for increments of pay and promotion purpose. 


SUDERSHAN KUMAR 
Under Secretary
